Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the optimization object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 39. The moduli space has dimension at most 176 with 1 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to optimization theory, we decompose the problem into 39 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 176. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the optimization space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 140 and the convergence rate is O(825/n^5).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 140 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the optimization structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 825. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 218.3 (Optimality Condition)
